Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the ode structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 890. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 824 and converges at rate O(1/n^2).

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to ode structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 890/824 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 212.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any ode module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 28. The projective resolution has length at most 825 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 28 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the ode structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 825. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 212.3 (Optimality Condition)

Proof: The optimality condition estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 169 + O(1/n^461). Summing over all modes and applying the ode optimality condition criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
